The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution of face 6 to the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>?</p>	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(10 &minus; 10)<sup>2</sup></sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">10</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">0</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">10</sub></span> = <strong>0.00</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(9 &minus; The <span style="color: #0247fe;">null hypothesis (H<sub>0</sub>)</span> is that each face has probability <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">1</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> on every roll.</p><p>Which calculation gives the contribution of face 4 to the <span style='color: #935d00;'>chi-square statistic (&chi;<sup>2</sup>)</span>?</p>	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">3<sup>2</sup> &minus; 5<sup>2</sup></sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">-16</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <strong>-3.20</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(3 &minus; 5)</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">-2</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <strong>-0.40</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(3 &minus; 5)<sup>2</sup></sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">30</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">4</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">30</sub></span> &asymp; <strong>0.13</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(3 &minus; 5)<sup>2</sup></sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">4</sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">6</sub></span> &asymp; <strong>0.67</strong>	Incorrect	<span style="display: inline-block; white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.8;"><sup style="font-size: 90%;">(3 &minus; 5)<sup>2</sup></sup>&frasl;<sub style="font-size: 90%;">5</sub></span> = <span style="display: inline-block;
